{"lang_code":"en","pos":"phrase","senses":[{"glosses":["Used with an interrogative pronoun as an intensifier to express anger, frustration, incredulity, or other strong emotion."],"tags":["slang","vulgar"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Shut the fuck up!","type":"example"},{"text":"Get the fuck out!","type":"example"},{"text":"Calm the fuck down!","type":"example"},{"text":"Back the fuck off!","type":"example"},{"text":"Stay the fuck away from me!","type":"example"},{"text":"Hurry the fuck up.","type":"example"},{"text":"Let's get the fuck to work.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used after verbs forming a part of a phrasal verb as an intensifier to imply aggressive emphasis."],"tags":["slang","vulgar"]},{"glosses":["Used as a shortened form of the common interrogative phrases."],"qualifier":"by ellipsis","tags":["slang","vulgar"]},{"examples":[{"text":"—Mom, I'm going out with my friends tonight. —The fuck you are!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to emphatically express that something isn't true."],"tags":["slang","vulgar"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I'm feeling the fuck good today.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to emphasize an adjective; fucking."],"qualifier":"African-American Vernacular","tags":["slang","vulgar"]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":486240,"upstream_offset":2275305668,"upstream_record_sha256":"332e189822f26c0c29f5413e709b9ee96555c7df55f61bfa54e4c12e812593c5","word":"the fuck"}
